5 Realisation of recipient

6 Length of recipient

7 Realisation and length

dative <- dative |> 
  mutate(
    Realisation_num = ifelse(RealizationOfRecipient == "NP", 0, 1)
  )

dative |> 
  ggplot(aes(LengthOfRecipient, Realisation_num)) +
  geom_smooth(
    method = "glm",
    method.args = list(family = "binomial"),
    colour = "sienna"
  ) +
  ylim(0, 1)

8 Regression model

dat_bm <- brm(
  RealizationOfRecipient ~ LengthOfRecipient,
  family = "bernoulli",
  data = dative,
  seed = 3926,
  file = "cache/dat_bm"
)

summary(dat_bm)
 Family: bernoulli 
  Links: mu = logit 
Formula: RealizationOfRecipient ~ LengthOfRecipient 
   Data: dative (Number of observations: 3263) 
  Draws: 4 chains, each with iter = 2000; warmup = 1000; thin = 1;
         total post-warmup draws = 4000

Regression Coefficients:
                  Estimate Est.Error l-95% CI u-95% CI Rhat Bulk_ESS Tail_ESS
Intercept            -2.35      0.08    -2.50    -2.19 1.00     3725     2796
LengthOfRecipient     0.70      0.04     0.63     0.78 1.00     3301     2502

Draws were sampled using sampling(NUTS). For each parameter, Bulk_ESS
and Tail_ESS are effective sample size measures, and Rhat is the potential
scale reduction factor on split chains (at convergence, Rhat = 1).

9 Posterior distributions

10 Odd ratio of the effect of length

dat_bm_draws <- as_draws_df(dat_bm) |> 
  mutate(
    Length_OR = exp(b_LengthOfRecipient)
  )

dat_bm_draws |> 
  ggplot(aes(Length_OR)) +
  geom_density(fill = "aquamarine4", alpha = 0.5) +
  labs(x = "Odd ratio of the effect of recipient length")

. . .

90% 80% 70% 60% 40%
[1.9, 2.15] [1.92, 2.12] [1.94, 2.1] [1.95, 2.09] [1.98, 2.06]

11 From 1 to 2 to 3 words

dat_bm_draws <- dat_bm_draws |> 
  mutate(
    p_1 = plogis(b_Intercept + b_LengthOfRecipient),
    p_2 = plogis(b_Intercept + b_LengthOfRecipient * 2),
    p_3 = plogis(b_Intercept + b_LengthOfRecipient * 3),
    p_2_1 = p_2 - p_1, p_3_2 = p_3 - p_2,
  )

quantile2(dat_bm_draws$p_2_1, probs = c(0.05, 0.95)) |> round(2)
  q5  q95 
0.11 0.13 
quantile2(dat_bm_draws$p_3_2, probs = c(0.05, 0.95)) |> round(2)
  q5  q95 
0.14 0.18

12 Expected values

library(tidybayes)

pred_grid <- tibble(
  LengthOfRecipient = seq(
    min(dative$LengthOfRecipient),
    max(dative$LengthOfRecipient)
  )
)

dat_bm_epred <- epred_draws(dat_bm, pred_grid) |> 
  mutate(
    median = median(.epred),
    lower = quantile2(.epred, probs = 0.025),
    upper = quantile2(.epred, probs = 0.975)
  )

dat_bm_epred |> 
  ggplot(aes(LengthOfRecipient, median)) +
  geom_ribbon(aes(ymin = lower, ymax = upper), alpha = 0.2) +
  geom_line(linewidth = 1, colour = "sienna")

13 Results

  • At 90% confidence, the probability of the recipient being realised as PP increases by a factor of 1.9 to 2.15 for each extra word in the recipient.

  • The probability of a PP increases by 11-13 percentage points from 1 to 2 words, at 90% probability. (But it is still below 50%, so an NP is still more likely).

  • Above 10 words, an PP is virtually certain.
